```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-055-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designStress ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "A single design stress in MPa is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:permissibleStress ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "A single permissible stress in MPa is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengthCriterionStatus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The Table 9 strength criteria must be satisfied."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Design stress must not exceed permissible stress." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designStress ?design .
                ?design qudt:numericValue ?designValue ;
                        qudt:unit unit:MegaPA .
                $this nltl:permissibleStress ?permissible .
                ?permissible qudt:numericValue ?permissibleValue ;
                             qudt:unit unit:MegaPA .
                FILTER (?designValue > ?permissibleValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-055> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designStress ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:permissibleStress ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengthCriterionStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The design stress must not exceed the permissible stress." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designStress ?designQuantity .
                ?designQuantity qudt:numericValue ?designValue .
                $this nltl:permissibleStress ?permissibleQuantity .
                ?permissibleQuantity qudt:numericValue ?permissibleValue .
                FILTER (
                    DATATYPE(?designValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    DATATYPE(?permissibleValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    ?designValue > ?permissibleValue
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaN>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The externally computed second candidate bow ice force is required in MN."
    ] .
```

Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:I2_048_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:polarClassValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarClassPc1
            nltl:polarClassPc2
            nltl:polarClassPc3
            nltl:polarClassPc4
            nltl:polarClassPc5
            nltl:polarClassPc6
            nltl:polarClassPc7
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must have exactly one supported Polar Class."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSteelGradeRequirementCase ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:steelGradeRequirementCase ;
        sh:node gs:I2_048_CaseShape
    ] .

gs:I2_048_CaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:steelGradeRequirementCasePlating ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:plating ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each steel-grade requirement case must identify exactly one assessed plating."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tableReference ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:tableReferenceValue ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iacsUrI2Table8 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each steel-grade requirement case must reference IACS UR I2 Table 8."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each case must provide one as-built plate thickness in millimetres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:steelMaterialClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:steelMaterialClassValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:steelMaterialClassOne
            nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo
            nltl:steelMaterialClassThree
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each case must specify one supported steel material class."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:steelStrengthCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:steelStrengthCategoryValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory
            nltl:highTensileSteelCategory
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each case must specify one supported steel strength category."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:actualHullStructuralSteelGrade ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:steelGradeValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:steelGradeAh
            nltl:steelGradeB
            nltl:steelGradeD
            nltl:steelGradeDh
            nltl:steelGradeE
            nltl:steelGradeEh
            nltl:steelGradeFh
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each case must provide exactly one actual hull structural steel grade."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:steelGradeValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:steelGradeAh
            nltl:steelGradeB
            nltl:steelGradeD
            nltl:steelGradeDh
            nltl:steelGradeE
            nltl:steelGradeEh
            nltl:steelGradeFh
        ) ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The required grade must be the Table 8 grade selected by Polar Class, thickness, material class and strength category; the actual grade must be at least that grade within the same strength category, and non-applicable combinations must have no required grade." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                ?ship a nltl:ship ;
                      nltl:polarClass ?pc ;
                      nltl:hasSteelGradeRequirementCase $this .
                $this nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ?thicknessNode ;
                      nltl:steelMaterialClass ?material ;
                      nltl:steelStrengthCategory ?strength ;
                      nltl:actualHullStructuralSteelGrade ?actual .
                ?thicknessNode qudt:numericValue ?thickness .
                BIND(
                    IF(?thickness <= 10, 1,
                    IF(?thickness <= 15, 2,
                    IF(?thickness <= 20, 3,
                    IF(?thickness <= 25, 4,
                    IF(?thickness <= 30, 5,
                    IF(?thickness <= 35, 6,
                    IF(?thickness <= 40, 7,
                    IF(?thickness <= 45, 8,
                    IF(?thickness <= 50, 9, 0)))))))))
                    AS ?band
                )
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) AS ?pc13)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc15)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc45)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7)) AS ?pc67)

                BIND(
                    IF(
                        (
                            ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                            (
                                (?band = 1 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 6 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne)
                            )
                        ),
                        nltl:steelGradeB,
                        IF(
                            (
                                ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                (
                                    (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                )
                            ),
                            nltl:steelGradeD,
                            IF(
                                (
                                    ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                    (
                                        (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67)
                                    )
                                ),
                                nltl:steelGradeE,
                                IF(
                                    (
                                        ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                        (
                                            (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                            (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                            (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc67)
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    nltl:steelGradeAh,
                                    IF(
                                        (
                                            ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                            (
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                                (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        nltl:steelGradeDh,
                                        IF(
                                            (
                                                ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                                (
                                                    (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo && ?pc15) ||
                                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree)
                                                )
                                            ),
                                            nltl:steelGradeEh,
                                            IF(
                                                (
                                                    ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                                    ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
                                                    (
                                                        (?band >= 7 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc13) ||
                                                        (?band = 9 && (?pc13 || ?pc45))
                                                    )
                                                ),
                                                nltl:steelGradeFh,
                                                nltl:benchmarkEntity
                                            )
                                        )
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )
                    AS ?expected
                )

                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required .
                }

                OPTIONAL {
                    ?actual nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?actualRank .
                }

                FILTER(
                    ?expected = nltl:benchmarkEntity ||
                    !BOUND(?required) ||
                    ?required != ?expected ||
                    !BOUND(?actualRank) ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredRank .
                        FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredRank)
                    } ||
                    (
                        ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                        ?actual NOT IN (
                            nltl:steelGradeB,
                            nltl:steelGradeD,
                            nltl:steelGradeE
                        )
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                        ?actual NOT IN (
                            nltl:steelGradeAh,
                            nltl:steelGradeDh,
                            nltl:steelGradeEh,
                            nltl:steelGradeFh
                        )
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-085> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerDiameter ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide one propeller diameter quantity in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bollardPropellerSpeedKnown ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:or (
            [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:bollardPropellerSpeedKnown ;
                    sh:hasValue true
                ] ;
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:propellerRotationalSpeedAtMaximumContinuousRatingBollard ;
                    sh:minCount 1 ;
                    sh:maxCount 1 ;
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:node [
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:maxCount 1 ;
                            sh:datatype xsd:decimal
                        ] ;
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path qudt:unit ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:maxCount 1 ;
                            sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/REV-PER-MIN>
                        ]
                    ] ;
                    sh:message "When bollard propeller speed is known, the known MCR bollard rotational speed must be recorded in revolutions per minute."
                ]
            ]
            [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:bollardPropellerSpeedKnown ;
                    sh:hasValue false
                ] ;
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;
                    sh:minCount 1 ;
                    sh:class nltl:tableLookupCase ;
                    sh:node [
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path nltl:tableLookupApplied ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:maxCount 1 ;
                            sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                            sh:hasValue true
                        ] ;
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path nltl:lookupInputQuantity ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:node [
                                sh:property [
                                    sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                    sh:minCount 1 ;
                                    sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                    sh:datatype xsd:decimal
                                ]
                            ]
                        ] ;
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path nltl:tableReference ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:maxCount 1 ;
                            sh:hasValue nltl:traficomTable6Dash9
                        ] ;
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path nltl:lookupSelectionEvidence ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
                        ]
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:sparql [
                    sh:message "When bollard propeller speed is unknown, a Table 6-9 lookup case must use the ship's propeller diameter as its lookup input." ;
                    sh:select """
                        PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                        PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                        PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                        SELECT $this
                        WHERE {
                            $this nltl:bollardPropellerSpeedKnown false .
                            FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                                $this nltl:propellerDiameter ?diameter .
                                ?diameter qudt:numericValue ?shipValue .
                                $this nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?case .
                                ?case nltl:lookupInputQuantity ?input .
                                ?input qudt:numericValue ?inputValue .
                                FILTER (
                                    xsd:decimal(?shipValue) =
                                    xsd:decimal(?inputValue)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    """
                ]
            ]
        )
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:path nltl:bollardPropellerSpeedKnown ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerRotationalSpeedAtMaximumContinuousRatingBollard ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/REV-PER-MIN>
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The Table 6-9 bollard propeller speed at MCR must be provided in revolutions per minute."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:TRF085LookupCaseShape ;
                sh:message "When the bollard propeller speed is not known, a Table 6-9 lookup case is required."
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The Table 6-9 lookup input must equal the ship's propeller diameter." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bollardPropellerSpeedKnown false .
                $this nltl:propellerDiameter ?diameterQuantity .
                ?diameterQuantity qudt:numericValue ?diameterValue .
                $this nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?lookupCase .
                ?lookupCase nltl:lookupInputQuantity ?inputQuantity .
                ?inputQuantity qudt:numericValue ?inputValue .
                FILTER (?inputValue != ?diameterValue)
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The reported bollard propeller speed must equal the result selected by the Table 6-9 lookup." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bollardPropellerSpeedKnown false .
                $this nltl:propellerRotationalSpeedAtMaximumContinuousRatingBollard ?speedQuantity .
                ?speedQuantity qudt:numericValue ?speedValue .
                $this nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?lookupCase .
                ?lookupCase nltl:lookupResultQuantity ?resultQuantity .
                ?resultQuantity qudt:numericValue ?resultValue .
                FILTER (?resultValue != ?speedValue)
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF085LookupCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:tableLookupCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tableLookupApplied ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The lookup case must record that the table lookup was applied."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lookupInputQuantity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The Table 6-9 lookup case must contain its input quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lookupResultQuantity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The Table 6-9 lookup case must contain its selected result quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lookupSelectionEvidence ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
        ] ;
        sh:message "The Table 6-9 selection must have supporting evidence."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tableReference ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:traficomTable6Dash9 ;

    ] .
```

Original source text:
The engine output P is the total maximum output the propulsion machinery can continuously deliver to the propeller(s). If the output of the machinery is restricted by technical means or by any regulations applicable to the ship, P shall be taken as the restricted output. If additional power sources are available for propulsion power (e.g. shaft motors), in addition to the power of the main engine(s), they shall also be included in the total engine output.
Normalized requirement:
The engine output P is the maximum output the propulsion machinery can continuously deliver to the propeller(s). If the output is restricted by technical means or regulations, P shall be taken as the restricted output.

CASE TRF-017-P03
Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: Additional propulsion source is included in total engine output.

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:PowerQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue unit:KiloW ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:UnrestrictedPropulsionOutputBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionOutputRestrictionApplies ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Maximum continuous rating power must equal the unrestricted continuous propulsion machinery output plus any additional propulsion power sources when no restriction applies." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?p .
                ?p qudt:numericValue ?pv .
                $this nltl:propulsionMachineryContinuousOutput ?c .
                ?c qudt:numericValue ?cv .
                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:additionalPropulsionPower ?a .
                    ?a qudt:numericValue ?av .
                }
                BIND (COALESCE(?av, 0) AS ?additional)
                FILTER (?pv != (?cv + ?additional))
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:RestrictedPropulsionOutputBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionOutputRestrictionApplies ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:restrictedPropulsionOutput ;
        sh:node gen:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Maximum continuous rating power must equal the restricted propulsion output plus any additional propulsion power sources when a restriction applies." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?p .
                ?p qudt:numericValue ?pv .
                $this nltl:restrictedPropulsionOutput ?r .
                ?r qudt:numericValue ?rv .
                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:additionalPropulsionPower ?a .
                    ?a qudt:numericValue ?av .
                }
                BIND (COALESCE(?av, 0) AS ?additional)
                FILTER (?pv != (?rv + ?additional))
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF_017_Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ;
        sh:node gen:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionMachineryContinuousOutput ;
        sh:node gen:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:additionalPropulsionPower ;
        sh:node gen:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;

        sh:path nltl:propulsionOutputRestrictionApplies ;
```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propulsionOutputRestrictionApplies ?restriction ;
                       nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?maximumPower .
                ?maximumPower qudt:numericValue ?maximumValue ;
                              qudt:unit unit:KiloW .
                {
                    FILTER (?restriction = true)
                    $this nltl:restrictedPropulsionOutput ?comparisonPower .
                    ?comparisonPower qudt:numericValue ?comparisonValue ;
                                     qudt:unit unit:KiloW .
                    FILTER (?maximumValue != ?comparisonValue)

                UNION
                {
                    FILTER (?restriction = false)
                    $this nltl:propulsionMachineryContinuousOutput ?comparisonPower .
                    ?comparisonPower qudt:numericValue ?comparisonValue ;
                                     qudt:unit unit:KiloW .
                    FILTER (?maximumValue != ?comparisonValue)
                }
            }
        """
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF017Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionOutputRestrictionApplies ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionMachineryContinuousOutput ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloW ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloW ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:TRF017UnrestrictedCase
        gen:TRF017RestrictedCase
    ) .

gen:TRF017UnrestrictedCase a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionOutputRestrictionApplies ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "When propulsion output is unrestricted, maximum continuous rating power must equal propulsion machinery continuous output." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?pNode .
                ?pNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                $this nltl:propulsionMachineryContinuousOutput ?cNode .
                ?cNode qudt:numericValue ?c .
                FILTER (?p != ?c)
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF017RestrictedCase a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionOutputRestrictionApplies ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:restrictedPropulsionOutput ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloW ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "When propulsion output is restricted, maximum continuous rating power must equal restricted propulsion output." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?pNode .
                ?pNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                $this nltl:restrictedPropulsionOutput ?rNode .
                ?rNode qudt:numericValue ?r .
                FILTER (?p != ?r)
            }
        """
    ] .

```
